'./captions.js';\nconst timeline=await fetch('./timeline.json').then(r=>{if(!r.ok)throw new Error('timeline missing');return r.json();});\nvalidateTimeline(timeline);\nconst renderers={};\nfor(let n=1;n<=12;n++){\n const id=String(n).padStart(2,'0');\n renderers[id]=(await import(`./scenes/scene${id}.js`))[`renderScene${id}`];\n if(typeof renderers[id]!=='function')throw new Error(`Missing './assets/interior.js';\nexport const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));\nexport const ease=x=>{x=clamp(x);return x*x*(3-2*x);};\nconst esc=s=>String(s).replaceAll('&','&amp;').replaceAll('<','&lt;').replaceAll('>','&gt;');\nexport const rect=(x,y,w,h,fill,extra='')=>`<rect x=\"${x}\" y=\"${y}\" width=\"${w}\" height=\"${h}\" fill=\"${fill}\" ${extra}/>`;\nexport const path=(d,fill='none',extra='')=>`<path d=\"${d}\" fill=\"${fill}\" ${extra}/>`;\nexport const text=(x,y,s,size=32,fill='#EAF4FF',extra='')=>`<text x=\"${x}\" y=\"${y}\" font-size=\"${size}\" fill=\"${fill}\" font-family=\"Kanit\" ${extra}>${esc(s)}</text>`;\nexport const ${960+dy*p*k}) scale(${1+(z-1)*(.88+.12*k)}) translate(${-cx} ${-cy})\">${s}</g>`;\n return layer(back,.45)+layer(mid,.85)+layer(front,1.3);\n}\n\nexport function cloth(t,{x=560,y=1000,scale=1,travel=0}={}){\n const sway=Math.sin(t*1.1)*4,drift=Math.sin(t*.6)*3;\n return `<g transform=\"translate(${x+travel+drift} ${y}) scale(${scale}) rotate(${sway*.3})\">\n sceneFor(t,scenes){\n const v=Math.max(0,Math.min(60-1e-7,Number(t)||0));\n const s=scenes.find(x=>v>=x.start&&v<x.end);\n if(!s)throw new Error(`No scene at${v}`);\n return s;\n}\nexport function validateTimeline(scenes){\n if(scenes.length!==12)throw new Error('Exactly12 scenes required');\n let next=0;\n for(const s of scenes){if(Math.abs(s.start-next)>1e-4||!(s.end>s.start))throw new Error(`Timeline gap/overlap at${s.id}`);next=s.end;}\n if(Math.abs(next-60)>1e-4)throw new Error(`Timeline ends at${next}`);\n return true;\n}\n\n\n===== build.ps1 './assets/interior.js';\nexport const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));\nexport const ease=x=>{x=clamp(x);return x*x*(3-2*x);};\nconst sceneFor(t,scenes){\n const v=Math.max(0,Math.min(60-1e-7,Number(t)||0));\n const s=scenes.find(x=>v>=x.start&&v<x.end);\n if(!s)throw new Error(`No scene at${v}`);\n return s;\n}\nexport function validateTimeline(scenes){\n if(scenes.length!==12)throw new Error('Exactly12 scenes required');\n let next=0;\n for(const s of scenes){if(Math.abs(s.start-next)>1e-4||!(s.end>s.start))throw new
